# Deadline trades — Couch To Front Office v10.3

32 fresh Boston franchises. 366 AI-to-AI deadline trades. 192 incoming calls to Boston.

Ratings against real NHL deadline prices in `ref/MARKET.md`: **realistic 366**, **questionable 0**, **absurd 0**. The write-up of totals, patterns, and the worst deals is in `ANALYSIS.md` next to this file.

Trades that include a 1st: 14. Trades that include two or more 1sts: 1. Trades that move a star (engine star, or 90+ OVR): 2.
